{"type":"assistant","message":{"model":"claude-opus-5","id":"msg_011CeQ9uDBqw3bi2n8XDiY2p","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_019pFGPmbLXvxDsyN48W4Mgd","name":"Bash","input":{"command":"cd /var/folders/x6/7ftjsrq95yg91h14lv_x5hpm0000gn/T/nurb-eval-trial-yqyqvxyf/project && cat > parts/bundle_holder.md <<'EOF'\n# bundle_holder\n\n## What it is\n\nA wall hook for a horizontal cable bundle. A flat back plate goes against the wall\nand takes one M4 pan-head screw; below the screw a short band juts forward, floors\nthe bundle and turns up into a lip so the bundle cannot roll off. The bundle threads\nalong the hook rather than dropping in from above, so the top is left open.\n\nThe bundle measures 8.0mm across (`measurements.toml`, calipers). Everything else is\nderived from that number, so the same file rebuilds for a fatter or thinner bundle.\n\n## Design notes\n\n- **Orientation.** Back face at minimum X against the wall, bundle along Y, down is\n  -Z. The part prints exactly as it mounts: every face is vertical, horizontal or a\n  45 degree chamfer, so it needs no supports and sits on a 130mm2 first layer.\n- **Why the pocket is 8.4 wide.** Bundle plus 0.4 of clearance, which is the free-fit\n  end of the fits table: a cable bundle is soft and irregular and never wants a\n  measured fit.\n- **Why the lip stops at 10.0.** A bundle can only sit 1mm off the floor and still be\n  blocked downward, so the lip only has to reach a little past the highest seat it\n  can hold. Taking it higher is material that blocks nothing.\n- **Why the screw sits so high.** The screw head and its driver need an 8.4 cylinder\n  in front of the plate. Parking it a clear 0.8 above the highest bundle the hook can\n  hold is what keeps a driver off the cables; the back plate's height falls out of\n  that, and height in the wall plane is nearly free.\n- **Cantilever.** The hook reaches 11.4 forward off a 23.4 tall back, a ratio of 0.5,\n  which the load-path table answers with chamfers alone. No gussets.\n- **Wall 3.0, not 2.0.** The lip's top face is one wall thick and lives between two\n  chamfered edges, and OCCT needs more than twice the chamfer there. 3.0 clears a\n  1.2 chamfer with room; 2.0 did not, and the 1.0 chamfer that fit left corner\n  triangles under the 1mm2 sliver floor.\n- **Chamfer 1.2, not 1.0.** Three chamfers meeting at a convex corner leave a\n  triangle of about `0.866 * size ** 2`. At 1.0 that is 0.87mm2, a sliver finding on\n  every one of them; at 1.2 it is 1.25mm2 and the part checks clean with no baseline\n  to accept. One size on every edge on the part.\n\n## Don't\n\n- **Don't roof the pocket into a closed tunnel.** It was the first shape tried, as a\n  teardrop so the roof would print. It works, but the roof pushes the screw another\n  5mm up the plate and costs more than it holds: the floor already stops the bundle\n  going down and the lip already stops it going forward, and the -Z limit alone pins\n  where the bundle can sit.\n- **Don't chamfer the screw bore.** Both bore rims are excluded from polish on\n  purpose. The back rim lies in the wall face, and a chamfer on the front rim eats\n  the flat the screw head has to seat on.\n- **Don't counterbore the screw.** The plate is one back_thickness deep and the head\n  is meant to sit proud on the front face. A pocket here would leave under 2.4 of\n  material along the bore, which is less than the screw needs to pull against.\n- **Don't drop the wall back to 2.0 or the chamfer back to 1.0** to save material.\n  See the design notes: both reintroduce findings, and the part is already inside its\n  volume target.\n- **Don't take the lip higher to \"hold better\".** It blocks nothing above 10.0 and\n  starts crowding the screw driver.\n\n## Changelog\n\n- 2026-08-25: first version. Open-top hook, single M4, 1286mm3.\n\n```toml\n[part]\nmin_wall = 3.0\nforward = [1, 0, 0]\n\n[variants.bundle_holder_thin]\nnote = \"The same hook sized for a slim 5mm bundle: a couple of stray cables rather than a loom.\"\n\n[variants.bundle_holder_thin.params]\nbundle_diameter = 5.0\n\n[variants.bundle_holder_fat]\nnote = \"The same hook opened up for a 12mm bundle, the thickest it holds before it needs a wider band.\"\n\n[variants.bundle_holder_fat.params]\nbundle_diameter = 12.0\n```\nEOF\nnurb check bundle_holder && nurb card bundle_holder && nurb verify bundle_holder","description":"Write card, check variants, verify"},"caller":{"type":"direct"}}],"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":2,"cache_creation_input_tokens":667,"cache_read_input_tokens":81995,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":667},"output_tokens":3,"service_tier":"standard","inference_geo":"not_available"},"diagnostics":null,"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"45ffeb01-9092-443a-9ba7-296fca2ddc2d","uuid":"f822b372-ab08-4f17-9594-36ea8fa4b8dd","timestamp":"2026-08-25T21:11:48.542Z","request_id":"req_011CeQ9uCDoZn74iycK892u4"}
